width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg><span class="grid"><span aria-hidden="true" class="invisible col-start-1 row-start-1">Copy page</span><span class="col-start-1 row-start-1">Copy page</span></span></div></button><button type="button" tabindex="0" aria-haspopup="menu" aria-expanded="false" id="base-ui-_R_dj36qqnpfb39p7b9snpfiutb_" aria-label="More actions" class="group disabled:pointer-events-none [&amp;&gt;span]:line-clamp-1 overflow-hidden group focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:-outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-primary dark:focus-visible:outline-primary-light py-0.5 gap-1 text-sm text-gray-950/50 dark:text-white/50 group-hover:text-gray-950/70 dark:group-hover:text-white/70 rounded-none rounded-r-xl border flex items-center justify-center h-[34px] border-gray-200 aspect-square dark:border-white/[0.07] bg-background-light dark:bg-background-dark hover:bg-gray-600/5 dark:hover:bg-gray-200/5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" class="size-3 transition-transform text-gray-400 group-hover:text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-600 dark:group-hover:text-gray-400 shrink-0 rotate-90"><path d="M6.5 2.75L12.75 9L6.5 15.25" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></button></div></header><div class="mdx-content @container/columns-container relative mt-8 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert [contain:inline-size] isolate empty:hidden mb-14" data-page-title="Swift" data-page-href="/architecture/swift" id="content"><span data-as="p">Swift is Mayan’s intent-based method for cross-chain swaps, and the path most orders take. Rather than bridging tokens or minting a wrapped asset, drivers deliver the user’s output directly on the destination chain from liquidity they already hold, and Swift releases the user’s original funds on the source chain afterwards.</span>
<span data-as="p">That ordering is what makes it fast. Swift settles in as little as 2 seconds, because the user is paid before the cross-chain message is verified rather than after the source chain finalizes.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="how-it-works"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#how-it-works" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">How it works</span></h2>
<div role="list" class="steps ml-3.5 mt-10 mb-6"><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-gray-200/70 dark:bg-white/10" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div data-component-part="step-indicator" class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="[[data-component-part=step-indicator]:has(a:focus-visible)_&amp;]:opacity-0">1</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">The user signs on the source chain</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">The source contract locks the input tokens. The input may first be swapped into the primary locked asset on that chain, typically USDC or ETH.</span></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-gray-200/70 dark:bg-white/10" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div data-component-part="step-indicator" class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="[[data-component-part=step-indicator]:has(a:focus-visible)_&amp;]:opacity-0">2</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">Drivers compete to fill it</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">Drivers submit offers on Solana based on the output they can deliver on the destination chain. The best offer wins.</span></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-gray-200/70 dark:bg-white/10" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div data-component-part="step-indicator" class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="[[data-component-part=step-indicator]:has(a:focus-visible)_&amp;]:opacity-0">3</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">The winning driver pays the user</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">They fulfil the swap directly on the destination chain from their own liquidity. The user has their funds at this point.</span></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-transparent bg-linear-to-b from-gray-200 dark:from-white/10 via-80% to-transparent [.step-container:has([data-component-part=step-content]:empty)_&amp;]:hidden" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div data-component-part="step-indicator" class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="[[data-component-part=step-indicator]:has(a:focus-visible)_&amp;]:opacity-0">4</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">The driver is reimbursed</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">The destination contract posts fulfilment data, which is executed on the source chain, and Swift emits an unlock message releasing the locked funds.</span></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">For payload-enabled swaps the destination contract stages the payout and completes it after a settlement step, which lets the payload be validated before final payout. The source unlock still happens after the VAA.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="what-it-gives-you"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#what-it-gives-you" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">What it gives you</span></h2>
<ul>
<li>Settlement in as little as 2 seconds, because the driver fronts the delivery rather than waiting on the source chain.</li>
<li>No bridge liquidity to seed, no wrapping, and no mint or burn mechanics.</li>
<li>Exact-out settlement on eligible assets. See <a class="link" href="/features/guaranteed-price">Guaranteed Price</a>.</li>
<li>Integration through the <a class="link" href="/build/sdk">SDK</a>, with minimal onchain assumptions.</li>
<li>Optional referrer fees for integrators. See <a class="link" href="/build/fees-earning">Fees &amp; Earning</a>.</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="transaction-size"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#transaction-size" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Transaction size</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Swift carries single transfers up to around $1M, depending on the chain. For larger transfers you can select <a class="link" href="/architecture/mctp">MCTP</a> instead, which carries up to around $10M.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="protocol-fee"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#protocol-fee" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Protocol fee</span></h2>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Swift has no protocol fee.</strong> The live value for any quote is still returned in the quote response, so read it rather than hardcoding.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="refunds"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#refunds" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Refunds</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">If the input was converted into the primary locked asset before the order opened, a refunded order returns that converted asset rather than the token the user sent. Surface this in your interface. See <a class="link" href="/how-mayan-works/refunds">Refunds</a>.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="contract-addresses"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#contract-addresses" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Contract addresses</span></h2>

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="earlier-versions"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#earlier-versions" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Earlier versions</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Everything above describes Swift as it works today. The detail below matters only if you integrated before the current version and have not migrated.</span>
<details class="accordion border-standard rounded-2xl mb-3 overflow-hidden bg-background-light dark:bg-codeblock cursor-default"><summary class="relative not-prose flex flex-row items-center content-center w-full cursor-pointer list-none [&amp;::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden gap-3 px-5 py-3.5 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:dark:bg-gray-800" aria-controls="migrating-from-the-original-swift-contracts-accordion-children" aria-expanded="false" data-component-part="accordion-button"><div id="migrating-from-the-original-swift-contracts" class="absolute top-[-4.5rem] [[data-banner-state=visible]_&amp;]:top-[-7rem]"></div><div class="flex size-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center" data-component-part="accordion-caret-right"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="currentColor" stroke="none" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0 text-gray-600 transition-transform duration-200 dark:text-gray-400 -rotate-90"><path d="M14.0238 4.00002H3.97576C3.33776 4.00002 2.74976 4.34702 2.44276 4.90602C2.13576 5.46502 2.15576 6.14802 2.49776 6.68702L7.52176 14.61C7.84476 15.119 8.39676 15.422 8.99976 15.422C9.60276 15.422 10.1548 15.118 10.4778 14.61L15.5028 6.68602C15.8438 6.14702 15.8648 5.46402 15.5578 4.90502C15.2508 4.34602 14.6628 3.99902 14.0248 3.99902L14.0238 4.00002Z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></div><div class="leading-tight text-left w-full min-w-0" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="accordion-title-container"><p id="migrating-from-the-original-swift-contracts-accordion-title" class="m-0 font-medium leading-6 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" data-component-part="accordion-title">Migrating from the original Swift contracts</p></div></summary><div id="migrating-from-the-original-swift-contracts-accordion-children" role="region" aria-labelledby="migrating-from-the-original-swift-contracts-accordion-title" class="px-5 pb-3.5 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto cursor-default" data-component-part="accordion-content"><span data-as="p">The original Swift used a single contract per chain. The current version splits that into a source contract and a destination contract, which is what allows the staging and settle step for payload swaps and gives more consistent settlement across chains.</span><span data-as="p">Two changes affect integrators:</span><ul>
<li><strong>Referrer fees moved.</strong> They were collected from the output token on the destination chain. They are now collected from the locked source assets and paid on the source chain.</li>
<li><strong>Payload handling changed.</strong> Payload swaps are now staged and settled in two steps, so the payload is validated before final payout.</li>
</ul><span data-as="p">The original contracts are not supported long term. Existing integrations should migrate.</span><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area"
